     <td><input class="projectionInvoiceUnit" data-index="${i}" type="number" min="0" step=".01" value="${unit.toFixed(2)}" oninput="recalcProjectionInvoiceModal()"></td>
   </tr>`;
 }).join("");

 openModal("Finalizar nota — venda total ou parcial",`
   <div class="panel" style="margin-top:0;background:#fff8fb">
    <div class="grid">
      <div class="field"><label>Cliente original do pedido</label><input value="${esc(originalClient)}" disabled></div>
      <div class="field"><label>Cliente que vai receber esta nota</label><select id="projectionInvoiceClient" onchange="updateProjectionInvoiceClientPrices()">${clientOptions}</select><div class="sub">Pode escolher outro cliente sem alterar o pedido original.</div></div>
      <div class="field"><label>Data de emissão da nota</label><input id="projectionInvoiceIssueDate" type="date" value="${isoDate(new Date())}"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>Vencimento</label><input id="projectionInvoiceDue" type="date" value="${selected[0]?.item.date||isoDate(new Date())}"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>Condição de pagamento</label><select id="projectionInvoiceTerms" onchange="toggleProjectionInvoiceTermsDetail()"><option>À vista</option><option>7 dias</option><option>14 dias</option><option>21 dias</option><option>28 dias</option><option>30 dias</option><option>Cheque</option><option>Personalizado</option></select></div>
      <div class="field" id="projectionInvoiceTermsDetailField" style="display:none"><label id="projectionInvoiceTermsDetailLabel">Detalhes da condição</label><input id="projectionInvoiceTermsDetail" placeholder="Informe os detalhes da condição de pagamento"></div>
    </div>
   </div>
   <div class="sub" style="margin:10px 0">Em <b>Qtd. nesta nota</b>, informe somente o que está saindo agora. O restante continua disponível na Projeção para outra nota ou outro cliente.</div>
   <div style="overflow:auto"><table><thead><tr><th>Produto</th><th>Original</th><th>Já faturado</th><th>Disponível</th><th>Qtd. nesta nota</th><th>Valor/peça</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></div>
   <div class="cards" style="margin-top:12px">
     <div class="card"><small>Peças nesta nota</small><strong id="projectionInvoiceQtyTotal">0</strong></div>
     <div class="card"><small>Valor desta nota</small><strong id="projectionInvoiceValueTotal">R$ 0,00</strong></div>
   </div>
   <button type="button" class="primary modalSave">Finalizar esta nota</button>`,()=>{
     let targetClientId=+document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceClient")?.value||null;
     let targetClient=(db.clients||[]).find(c=>+c.id===+targetClientId);
     if(!targetClient){alert("Selecione o cliente que receberá a nota.");return}
     let lineInputs=[...document.querySelectorAll(".projectionInvoiceQty")];
     let invoiceItems=[],totalValue=0,totalQty=0;
     lineInputs.forEach(inp=>{
       let i=+inp.dataset.index,ctx=selected[i];if(!ctx)return;
       let available=ctx.item.remainingQty!=null?ctx.item.remainingQty:Math.max(0,ctx.item.qty-(ctx.item.invoicedQty||0));
       let q=Math.max(0,Math.min(available,+inp.value||0));
       let unit=+document.querySelector(`.projectionInvoiceUnit[data-index="${i}"]`)?.value||0;
       if(q>0){invoiceItems.push({ctx,qty:q,unit,value:q*unit});totalQty+=q;totalValue+=q*unit}
     });
     if(!invoiceItems.length){alert("Informe pelo menos uma quantidade para esta nota.");return}

     db.finance=db.finance||[];db.projectionInvoices=db.projectionInvoices||[];
     let issueDate=document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceIssueDate")?.value||isoDate(new Date());
     let due=document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceDue")?.value||issueDate;
     let terms=document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceTerms")?.value||"À vista";
     let termsDetail=document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceTermsDetail")?.value?.trim()||"";
     if(terms==="Personalizado"&&!termsDetail){alert("Informe os detalhes da condição de pagamento personalizada.");return}
     let invoiceId=Date.now();

     db.projectionInvoices.push({
       id:invoiceId,
       client:targetClient.name,
       clientId:targetClient.id,
       sourceClient:originalClient,
       items:invoiceItems.map(x=>({orderId:x.ctx.o.id,itemKey:x.ctx.item.key,productName:x.ctx.item.name,productId:x.ctx.item.productId||null,projectionDate:x.ctx.item.date||x.ctx.o.date||"",deliveredAt:issueDate,qty:x.qty,unitPrice:x.unit,value:x.value})),
       date:issueDate,issueDate:issueDate,dueDate:due,value:totalValue,terms,termsDetail,status:"Pendente"
     });
     db.finance.push({
       id:invoiceId+1,type:"Receber",
       desc:`Nota projeção — ${targetClient.name} — ${invoiceItems.map(x=>x.ctx.item.name).join(", ")}`,
       value:totalValue,status:"Pendente",paid:0,remaining:totalValue,date:due,dueDate:due,issueDate:issueDate,paymentTerms:terms,paymentTermsDetail:termsDetail,
       clientId:targetClient.id,clientName:targetClient.name,projectionInvoiceId:invoiceId
     });

     invoiceItems.forEach(({ctx,qty})=>{
       let o=ctx.o,item=ctx.item;
       o.projectionItems=o.projectionItems||{};
       let saved=o.projectionItems[item.key]||{};
       let prev=saved.invoicedQty!=null?(+saved.invoicedQty||0):(saved.invoiced?item.qty:0);
       let newQty=Math.min(item.qty,prev+qty);
       o.projectionItems[item.key]={
         ...saved,date:item.date,invoicedQty:newQty,invoiced:newQty>=item.qty,
         invoiceIds:[...((saved.invoiceIds)||[]),invoiceId],
         deliveryHistory:[...((saved.deliveryHistory)||[]),{invoiceId,date:issueDate,qty}]
       };
       let all=projectionItemsForOrder(o);
       o.projectionInvoiced=all.every(i=>i.invoiced);
     });

     closeModal();
     save();
     renderProjection();
     renderFinance();
     alert(`Nota finalizada para ${targetClient.name}: ${totalQty.toLocaleString("pt-BR")} peça(s) — ${money(totalValue)}. O saldo não faturado continua disponível.`);
   });
 setTimeout(()=>{updateProjectionInvoiceClientPrices();recalcProjectionInvoiceModal()},30);
}
function updateProjectionInvoiceClientPrices(){
 let clientId=+document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceClient")?.value||0;
 let keys=[...document.querySelectorAll(".projectionSelect:checked")].map(x=>x.value);
 let selected=keys.map(v=>{let [oid,key]=String(v).split("::"),o=(db.orders||[]).find(x=>+x.id===+oid);if(!o)return null;let item=projectionItemsForOrder(o).find(i=>String(i.key)===key);return item?{o,item}:null}).filter(Boolean);
 document.querySelectorAll(".projectionInvoiceUnit").forEach(inp=>{
   let i=+inp.dataset.index,ctx=selected[i];if(!ctx)return;
   let unit=0;
   if(ctx.item.productId&&clientId){try{unit=+suggestedPrice(clientId,ctx.item.productId)||0}catch(e){}}
   if(!unit)unit=ctx.item.qty>0?(+ctx.item.value||0)/ctx.item.qty:0;
   inp.value=unit.toFixed(2);
 });
 recalcProjectionInvoiceModal();
}

function toggleProjectionInvoiceTermsDetail(){
 let sel=document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceTerms"),field=document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceTermsDetailField"),label=document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceTermsDetailLabel"),inp=document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceTermsDetail");
 if(!sel||!field)return;

 if(inp){
   inp.placeholder=value==="Cheque"?"Ex.: 2 cheques — 15/09 e 30/09":"Ex.: entrada + 2 parcelas, 10/20/30 dias";
   if(!show)inp.value="";
 }
}
function recalcProjectionInvoiceModal(){
 let qty=0,value=0;
 document.querySelectorAll(".projectionInvoiceQty").forEach(inp=>{
   let i=inp.dataset.index,q=Math.max(0,+inp.value||0),u=+document.querySelector(`.projectionInvoiceUnit[data-index="${i}"]`)?.value||0;
   qty+=q;value+=q*u;
 });
 let qel=document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceQtyTotal"),vel=document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceValueTotal");
 if(qel)qel.textContent=qty.toLocaleString("pt-BR");
 if(vel)vel.textContent=money(value);
}

function projectionRemainingQty(item){
 return Math.max(0,item?.remainingQty!=null?(+item.remainingQty||0):((+item?.qty||0)-(+item?.invoicedQty||0)));
}
function projectionUnitForItem(item){
 return (+item?.qty||0)>0?(+item?.value||0)/(+item?.qty||0):0;
}
function projectionRemainingValue(item){
 return projectionRemainingQty(item)*projectionUnitForItem(item);
}
function projectionOpenMissingQty(order,item){
 const oid=String(order?.id??'').trim();
 const pid=String(item?.productId??'').trim();
 if(!oid||!pid)return 0;
 return (db.missingPieces||[]).reduce((sum,m)=>{
   if(String(m?.orderId??'').trim()!==oid)return sum;
   if(String(m?.productId??'').trim()!==pid)return sum;
   if(String(m?.issueType||'Peça faltante')!=='Peça faltante')return sum;
   const allocated=(Array.isArray(m?.history)?m.history:[]).reduce((a,h)=>{
     const q=Math.max(0,+h?.qty||0),dest=String(h?.destination||'');
     if(dest==='finishedPieces')return a+q;
     if(dest==='clientInvoice')return a+Math.max(0,q-Math.max(0,+h?.projectionAppliedQty||0));
     return a;
   },0);
   return sum+Math.max(0,+m?.remainingQty||0)+allocated;
 },0);
}
function projectionDeliverableQty(order,item){
 return Math.max(0,projectionRemainingQty(item)-projectionOpenMissingQty(order,item));
}
function projectionDeliverableValue(order,item){
 return projectionDeliverableQty(order,item)*projectionUnitForItem(item);
}
function projectionDeliveredRows(startS="0000-01-01",endS="9999-12-31"){
 let out=[];
 (db.projectionInvoices||[]).forEach(inv=>{
   let date=String(inv.issueDate||inv.date||"").slice(0,10);
   if(!date||date<startS||date>endS)return;
   (inv.items||[]).forEach(it=>{
     let o=(db.orders||[]).find(x=>+x.id===+it.orderId)||null;
     out.push({invoice:inv,order:o,item:it,date,qty:+it.qty||0,value:+it.value||((+it.qty||0)*(+it.unitPrice||0))});
   });
 });
 return out;
}
function projectionTrackingMatches(order,productName){
 let clientF=(document.getElementById("projectionClientFilter")?.value||"").trim().toLowerCase();
 let productF=(document.getElementById("projectionProductFilter")?.value||"").trim().toLowerCase();
 let orderF=(document.getElementById("projectionOrderFilter")?.value||"").trim().toLowerCase();
 let displayNo=String(order?(typeof displayOrderNumber==="function"?displayOrderNumber(order):order.id):"").toLowerCase();
 if(clientF&&!String(order?.client||"").toLowerCase().includes(clientF))return false;
 if(productF&&!String(productName||"").toLowerCase().includes(productF))return false;
 if(orderF&&!displayNo.includes(orderF))return false;
 return true;
}

}

   </div>
   <div class="sub" style="margin:10px 0">Somente as ${remaining.toLocaleString("pt-BR")} peça(s) que ainda faltam serão movidas. O que já foi entregue permanece no histórico.</div>

}

function importBackupFile(input){
 if(!isOwnerUser()){if(input)input.value="";alert("Somente o proprietário principal pode importar backup.");return}
 let file=input?.files?.[0];if(!file)return;
 let reader=new FileReader();
 reader.onload=async()=>{
   try{
     let data=JSON.parse(String(reader.result||""));
     if(!data||typeof data!=="object")throw new Error("Backup inválido");
     if(!confirm("Importar este backup? Os dados atuais desta versão serão substituídos pelos dados do arquivo.")){input.value="";return}
     db=data;ensurePayrollData();ensureCloudUserAccess();localSaveOnly();input.value="";
     if(cloudAccessToken){
       await cloudUploadCurrentDb(false);
       alert("Backup importado e enviado para a nuvem com sucesso.");
     }else{alert("Backup importado com sucesso. Entre no sistema para enviá-lo à nuvem.");}
     location.reload();
   }catch(err){console.error(err);alert("Não foi possível importar o backup. Verifique se o arquivo JSON é um backup válido do sistema.");input.value=""}
 };
 reader.readAsText(file);
}

 production:"producao",productionLocations:"producao",capacityAssignments:"producao",goals:"producao",defectReturns:"producao",missingPieces:"producao",machines:"producao",serviceTypes:"producao",
 cuts:"corte",cutterPieceTypes:"corte",stock:"estoque",assets:"estoque",
 factions:"faccoes",factionMasters:"faccoes",factionPayments:"faccoes",cutters:"cortadores",

function hlgbRecordCanWrite(module){
  if(isOwnerUser())return true;
  const area=HLGB_RECORD_WRITE_AREA[module];
  return !!area&&hasAccess(area);
}
function hlgbRecordCanRead(module){
  if(isOwnerUser())return true;
  if(hlgbRecordReadable.has(module))return true;
  const direct=HLGB_RECORD_WRITE_AREA[module];
  if(direct&&hasAccess(direct))return true;
  const any=(...areas)=>areas.some(a=>hasAccess(a));
  if(module==="products")return any("produtos","pedidos","corte","producao","materiais","estoque","faccoes","projecao");
  if(module==="clients")return any("clientes","pedidos","producao","corte","faccoes","projecao","financeiro");
  if(module==="orders")return any("pedidos","producao","corte","faccoes","projecao");
  if(module==="materials")return any("materiais","produtos","corte","estoque");
  if(module==="colors"||module==="sizes")return any("cadastros","produtos","pedidos","corte");
  if(module==="productionLocations")return any("producao","corte","faccoes");
  if(module==="employees")return any("funcionarios","folhaPagamento","exFuncionarios");
  if(module==="suppliers")return any("fornecedores","financeiro","materiais");
  if(module==="cutters")return any("cortadores","corte","producao");
  if(module==="factionMasters"||module==="factions")return any("faccoes","producao");
  return false;
}

function hlgbRecordRowValue(row,module=""){
  if(!row)return null;
  return hlgbRecordHydrateData(module,row);
}
function hlgbRecordHydrateData(module,row){
  const d=hlgbRecordClone(row?.data);
  if(d&&typeof d==="object"&&!Array.isArray(d)&&d.id==null&&!d.__hlgbId&&!(module==="products"&&d.code))d.__hlgbId=String(row?.entity_id||"");
  return d;
}
function hlgbRecordSnapshotRows(module,rows){
  const m=new Map();let max="";
  (rows||[]).forEach(r=>{
    if(r?.entity_id==null)return;
    const data=hlgbRecordHydrateData(module,r);
    m.set(String(r.entity_id),{
      data,deleted_at:r.deleted_at||null,revision:+r.revision||1,updated_at:r.updated_at||"",updated_by:r.updated_by||null
    });
    if(String(r.updated_at||"")>max)max=String(r.updated_at||"");
  });
  hlgbRecordSnapshots[module]=m;if(max)hlgbRecordLastSeen[module]=max;
}

function hlgb924ProtectImplicitDelete(module,old){
  if(module==="projectionInvoices")return true;
  if(module==="finance"&&old?.data?.projectionInvoiceId!=null)return true;
  return false;
}
function hlgbRecordPendingStore(){
  if(!hlgbRecordReady||cloudApplying||cloudBootstrapping)return;
  try{
    const pending={at:Date.now(),email:normEmail(cloudUser?.email||""),modules:{}};let any=false;
    for(const module of HLGB_RECORD_MODULES){
      // Não deixa um usuário secundário ficar eternamente “pendente” por mudanças
      // internas de módulos que ele não tem permissão para gravar.
      if(!hlgbRecordCanWrite(module))continue;
      const snap=hlgbRecordSnapshots[module]||new Map(),local=hlgbRecordLocalMap(module),ops=[];
      for(const [id,item] of local){
        const old=snap.get(id);
        if(!old||old.deleted_at||!cloudEq(old.data,item)){ops.push({id,data:hlgbRecordClone(item),deleted:false});any=true}
      }
      // v91.26: ausência local nunca gera exclusão automática na nuvem.
      if(ops.length)pending.modules[module]=ops;
    }
    if(any)localStorage.setItem(HLGB_RECORD_PENDING_KEY,JSON.stringify(pending));else hlgbRecordPendingClear();
  }catch(e){console.warn("HLGB record pending",e)}
}
async function hlgbRecordLoadDirect(since=null){
  // Carga em pacotes pequenos: evita que o Safari interrompa uma resposta grande.
  // O RPC é SECURITY INVOKER, exige sessão autenticada e continua protegido por RLS.
  const pageSize=250,rows=[];let serverTime="";
  try{
    for(let offset=0;offset<12000;offset+=pageSize){
      let packet=await cloudRequest("rpc/hlgb_load_records_page",{method:"POST",body:JSON.stringify({p_offset:offset,p_limit:pageSize,p_since:since||null})});
      if(Array.isArray(packet)&&packet.length===1)packet=packet[0];
      const page=Array.isArray(packet?.rows)?packet.rows:[];
      if(!packet||typeof packet!=="object")throw new Error("O pacote paginado não respondeu corretamente.");
      rows.push(...page);serverTime=String(packet.server_time||serverTime||"");
      if(packet.has_more!==true||page.length<pageSize)break;
    }
  }catch(rpcPageError){
    console.warn("HLGB: RPC paginado indisponível; tentando leitura REST paginada.",rpcPageError);
    rows.length=0;
    for(let offset=0;offset<12000;offset+=pageSize){
      let filter=since?`&updated_at=gte.${encodeURIComponent(since)}`:"";
      let page=await cloudRequest(`hlgb_records?select=module,entity_id,data,deleted_at,revision,updated_at,updated_by${filter}&order=updated_at.asc&limit=${pageSize}&offset=${offset}`);
      if(!Array.isArray(page))throw new Error("A leitura REST paginada não retornou uma lista de registros.");
      rows.push(...page);if(page.length<pageSize)break;
    }
  }
  const visibleModules=[...new Set(rows.map(r=>String(r?.module||"")).filter(m=>HLGB_RECORD_MODULES.includes(m)))];
  const modules=normEmail(cloudUser?.email)===normEmail(HLGB_OWNER_EMAIL)?HLGB_RECORD_MODULES.slice():visibleModules;
  return {modules,rows,server_time:serverTime||new Date().toISOString(),fallback:true};
}
async function hlgbRecordLoadBundle({preserveLocal=false,since=null}={}){

  let bundle;

    // redes móveis interrompam o login ao receber mais de mil registros de uma vez.
    bundle=await hlgbRecordLoadDirect(since);
  }else{
    try{
      bundle=await cloudRequest("rpc/hlgb_load_bundle",{method:"POST",

          db.cuts=(db.cuts||[]).filter(cut=>+cut.orderId!==+o.id);

        }
        closeModal();save();try{renderOrders();renderCuts();renderProjection()}catch(e){}
      });
    setTimeout(()=>{hlgb922ClientChanged();hlgb922RecalcAdHoc()},20);
  }

  const originalEditOrder=window.editOrder;
  window.editOrder=function(id){
    let o=(db.orders||[]).find(x=>+x.id===+id);
    if(o&&o.isAdHoc&&o.noGrade)return editNoGradeAdHoc(o);
    return originalEditOrder(id);
  };
})();

<script>
// v91.23 — projection invoices are the authoritative source for delivered/remaining.
// The repair is additive only: it never reduces a saved delivery and never clears notes.
(function(){
  if(typeof projectionItemsForOrder!=="function")return;
  const _v9123BaseProjectionItemsForOrder=projectionItemsForOrder;
  const V9123_BACKUP_KEY="hlgb_projection_safety_backup";

  function clone(v){try{return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v))}catch(e){return v}}
  function invoiceId(v){return String(v==null?"":v)}
  function invoiceDate(inv){return String(inv?.issueDate||inv?.date||"").slice(0,10)}
  function financeForInvoice(id){return (db.finance||[]).find(f=>invoiceId(f.projectionInvoiceId)===invoiceId(id))||null}
  function itemMatches(orderId,item,inv,it){
    let oid=it?.orderId!=null?it.orderId:inv?.orderId;
    if(String(oid??"")!==String(orderId??""))return false;
    if(it?.itemKey!=null&&String(it.itemKey)!=="")return String(it.itemKey)===String(item?.key??"");
    if(item?.productId&&it?.productId)return +it.productId===+item.productId;
    return String(it?.productName||"").trim().toLowerCase()===String(item?.name||"").trim().toLowerCase();
  }
  function invoiceLinks(orderId,item){
    let out=[];
    (Array.isArray(db.projectionInvoices)?db.projectionInvoices:[]).forEach(inv=>{
      (Array.isArray(inv?.items)?inv.items:[]).forEach(it=>{
        if(itemMatches(orderId,item,inv,it))out.push({inv,it,qty:Math.max(0,+it.qty||0)});
      });
    });
    return out;
  }
  function currentProjectionStateScore(){
    let inv=(Array.isArray(db.projectionInvoices)?db.projectionInvoices:[]).length,h=0,refs=0;
    (db.orders||[]).forEach(o=>Object.values(o?.projectionItems||{}).forEach(x=>{
      h+=Array.isArray(x?.deliveryHistory)?x.deliveryHistory.length:0;
      refs+=Array.isArray(x?.invoiceIds)?x.invoiceIds.length:0;
    }));
    return inv*10000+h*10+refs;
  }
  function safetyBackup(){
    try{
      let payload={savedAt:new Date().toISOString(),score:currentProjectionStateScore(),projectionInvoices:clone(Array.isArray(db.projectionInvoices)?db.projectionInvoices:[]),orders:(db.orders||[]).map(o=>({id:o.id,orderNumber:o.orderNumber,client:o.client,clientId:o.clientId,date:o.date,projectionItems:clone(o.projectionItems||{}),projectionInvoiced:o.projectionInvoiced})),finance:clone((db.finance||[]).filter(f=>f?.projectionInvoiceId!=null))};
      let old=null;try{old=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(V9123_BACKUP_KEY)||"null")}catch(e){}
      if(!old||(+payload.score||0)>=(+old.score||0))localStorage.setItem(V9123_BACKUP_KEY,JSON.stringify(payload));
    }catch(e){console.warn("HLGB v91.23 backup da projeção",e)}
  }
  function referencedInvoiceIds(){
    let set=new Set();
    (db.finance||[]).forEach(f=>{if(f?.projectionInvoiceId!=null)set.add(invoiceId(f.projectionInvoiceId))});
    (db.orders||[]).forEach(o=>Object.values(o?.projectionItems||{}).forEach(x=>(x?.invoiceIds||[]).forEach(id=>set.add(invoiceId(id)))));
    return set;
  }
  function restoreFromSafetyBackup(){
    let old=null;try{old=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(V9123_BACKUP_KEY)||"null")}catch(e){}
    if(!old||!Array.isArray(old.projectionInvoices)||!old.projectionInvoices.length)return false;
    db.projectionInvoices=Array.isArray(db.projectionInvoices)?db.projectionInvoices:[];
    let existing=new Set(db.projectionInvoices.map(x=>invoiceId(x.id))),refs=referencedInvoiceIds(),changed=false;
    old.projectionInvoices.forEach(inv=>{
      let id=invoiceId(inv?.id);if(!id||existing.has(id)||!refs.has(id))return;
      db.projectionInvoices.push(clone(inv));existing.add(id);changed=true;
    });
    return changed;
  }
  function recoverMissingInvoicesFromOrders(){
    db.projectionInvoices=Array.isArray(db.projectionInvoices)?db.projectionInvoices:[];
    let existing=new Map(db.projectionInvoices.map(x=>[invoiceId(x.id),x])),groups=new Map();
    (db.orders||[]).forEach(o=>{
      let baseRows=_v9123BaseProjectionItemsForOrder(o)||[],byKey=new Map(baseRows.map(x=>[String(x.key),x]));
      Object.entries(o?.projectionItems||{}).forEach(([key,saved])=>{
        let row=byKey.get(String(key));if(!row)return;
        let ids=Array.isArray(saved?.invoiceIds)?saved.invoiceIds.map(invoiceId).filter(Boolean):[];
        let history=Array.isArray(saved?.deliveryHistory)?saved.deliveryHistory:[];
        let knownQty=0;
        ids.forEach(id=>{let inv=existing.get(id);if(inv)(inv.items||[]).forEach(it=>{if(itemMatches(o.id,row,inv,it))knownQty+=Math.max(0,+it.qty||0)})});
        let missing=ids.filter(id=>!existing.has(id));
        let remainingHistorical=Math.max(0,(+saved?.invoicedQty||0)-knownQty);
        missing.forEach((id,idx)=>{
          let hist=history.find(h=>invoiceId(h?.invoiceId)===id);

d===+(document.getElementById('mclient')?.value||0));
        let nl=hlgb922ReadAdHocLines();if(!nl.length){alert('Adicione pelo menos um produto com quantidade.');return}

<script>
// v91.23 — projection invoices are the authoritative source for delivered/remaining.
// The repair is additive only: it never reduces a saved delivery and never clears notes.
(function(){
  if(typeof projectionItemsForOrder!=="function")return;
  const _v9123BaseProjectionItemsForOrder=projectionItemsForOrder;
  const V9123_BACKUP_KEY="hlgb_projection_safety_backup";

  function clone(v){try{return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v))}catch(e){return v}}
  function invoiceId(v){return String(v==null?"":v)}
  function invoiceDate(inv){return String(inv?.issueDate||inv?.date||"").slice(0,10)}
  function financeForInvoice(id){return (db.finance||[]).find(f=>invoiceId(f.projectionInvoiceId)===invoiceId(id))||null}
  function itemMatches(orderId,item,inv,it){
    let oid=it?.orderId!=null?it.orderId:inv?.orderId;
    if(String(oid??"")!==String(orderId??""))return false;
    if(it?.itemKey!=null&&String(it.itemKey)!=="")return String(it.itemKey)===String(item?.key??"");
    if(item?.productId&&it?.productId)return +it.productId===+item.productId;
    return String(it?.productName||"").trim().toLowerCase()===String(item?.name||"").trim().toLowerCase();
  }
  function invoiceLinks(orderId,item){
    let out=[];
    (Array.isArray(db.projectionInvoices)?db.projectionInvoices:[]).forEach(inv=>{
      (Array.isArray(inv?.items)?inv.items:[]).forEach(it=>{
        if(itemMatches(orderId,item,inv,it))out.push({inv,it,qty:Math.max(0,+it.qty||0)});
      });
    });
    return out;
  }
  function currentProjectionStateScore(){
    let inv=(Array.isArray(db.projectionInvoices)?db.projectionInvoices:[]).length,h=0,refs=0;
    (db.orders||[]).forEach(o=>Object.values(o?.projectionItems||{}).forEach(x=>{
      h+=Array.isArray(x?.deliveryHistory)?x.deliveryHistory.length:0;
      refs+=Array.isArray(x?.invoiceIds)?x.invoiceIds.length:0;
    }));
    return inv*10000+h*10+refs;
  }
  function safetyBackup(){
    try{
      let payload={savedAt:new Date().toISOString(),score:currentProjectionStateScore(),projectionInvoices:clone(Array.isArray(db.projectionInvoices)?db.projectionInvoices:[]),orders:(db.orders||[]).map(o=>({id:o.id,orderNumber:o.orderNumber,client:o.client,clientId:o.clientId,date:o.date,projectionItems:clone(o.projectionItems||{}),projectionInvoiced:o.projectionInvoiced})),finance:clone((db.finance||[]).filter(f=>f?.projectionInvoiceId!=null))};
      let old=null;try{old=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(V9123_BACKUP_KEY)||"null")}catch(e){}

<script>
// v91.23 — projection invoices are the authoritative source for delivered/remaining.
// The repair is additive only: it never reduces a saved delivery and never clears notes.
(function(){
  if(typeof projectionItemsForOrder!=="function")return;
  const _v9123BaseProjectionItemsForOrder=projectionItemsForOrder;
  const V9123_BACKUP_KEY="hlgb_projection_safety_backup";

  function clone(v){try{return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v))}catch(e){return v}}
  function invoiceId(v){return String(v==null?"":v)}
  function invoiceDate(inv){return String(inv?.issueDate||inv?.date||"").slice(0,10)}
  function financeForInvoice(id){return (db.finance||[]).find(f=>invoiceId(f.projectionInvoiceId)===invoiceId(id))||null}
  function itemMatches(orderId,item,inv,it){
    let oid=it?.orderId!=null?it.orderId:inv?.orderId;
    if(String(oid??"")!==String(orderId??""))return false;
    if(it?.itemKey!=null&&String(it.itemKey)!=="")return String(it.itemKey)===String(item?.key??"");
    if(item?.productId&&it?.productId)return +it.productId===+item.productId;
    return String(it?.productName||"").trim().toLowerCase()===String(item?.name||"").trim().toLowerCase();
  }
  function invoiceLinks(orderId,item){
    let out=[];
    (Array.isArray(db.projectionInvoices)?db.projectionInvoices:[]).forEach(inv=>{
      (Array.isArray(inv?.items)?inv.items:[]).forEach(it=>{
        if(itemMatches(orderId,item,inv,it))out.push({inv,it,qty:Math.max(0,+it.qty||0)});
      });
    });
    return out;
  }
  function currentProjectionStateScore(){
    let inv=(Array.isArray(db.projectionInvoices)?db.projectionInvoices:[]).length,h=0,refs=0;
    (db.orders||[]).forEach(o=>Object.values(o?.projectionItems||{}).forEach(x=>{
      h+=Array.isArray(x?.deliveryHistory)?x.deliveryHistory.length:0;
      refs+=Array.isArray(x?.invoiceIds)?x.invoiceIds.length:0;
    }));
    return inv*10000+h*10+refs;
  }
  function safetyBackup(){
    try{
      let payload={savedAt:new Date().toISOString(),score:currentProjectionStateScore(),projectionInvoices:clone(Array.isArray(db.projectionInvoices)?db.projectionInvoices:[]),orders:(db.orders||[]).map(o=>({id:o.id,orderNumber:o.orderNumber,client:o.client,clientId:o.clientId,date:o.date,projectionItems:clone(o.projectionItems||{}),projectionInvoiced:o.projectionInvoiced})),finance:clone((db.finance||[]).filter(f=>f?.projectionInvoiceId!=null))};
      let old=null;try{old=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(V9123_BACKUP_KEY)||"null")}catch(e){}
      if(!old||(+payload.score||0)>=(+old.score||0))localStorage.setItem(V9123_BACKUP_KEY,JSON.stringify(payload));
    }catch(e){console.warn("HLGB v91.23 backup da projeção",e)}
  }
  function referencedInvoiceIds(){
    let set=new Set();
    (db.finance||[]).forEach(f=>{if(f?.projectionInvoiceId!=null)set.add(invoiceId(f.projectionInvoiceId))});
    (db.orders||[]).forEach(o=>Object.values(o?.projectionItems||{}).forEach(x=>(x?.invoiceIds||[]).forEach(id=>set.add(invoiceId(id)))));
    return set;
  }
  function restoreFromSafetyBackup(){
    let old=null;try{old=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(V9123_BACKUP_KEY)||"null")}catch(e){}
    if(!old||!Array.isArray(old.projectionInvoices)||!old.projectionInvoices.length)return false;
    db.projectionInvoices=Array.isArray(db.projectionInvoices)?db.projectionInvoices:[];

// v91.23 — projection invoices are the authoritative source for delivered/remaining.
// The repair is additive only: it never reduces a saved delivery and never clears notes.
(function(){
  if(typeof projectionItemsForOrder!=="function")return;
  const _v9123BaseProjectionItemsForOrder=projectionItemsForOrder;
  const V9123_BACKUP_KEY="hlgb_projection_safety_backup";

  function invoiceId(v){return String(v==null?"":v)}
  function invoiceDate(inv){return String(inv?.issueDate||inv?.date||"").slice(0,10)}
  function financeForInvoice(id){return (db.finance||[]).find(f=>invoiceId(f.projectionInvoiceId)===invoiceId(id))||null}
  function itemMatches(orderId,item,inv,it){
    let oid=it?.orderId!=null?it.orderId:inv?.orderId;
    if(String(oid??"")!==String(orderId??""))return false;
    if(it?.itemKey!=null&&String(it.itemKey)!=="")return String(it.itemKey)===String(item?.key??"");
    if(item?.productId&&it?.productId)return +it.productId===+item.productId;
    return String(it?.productName||"").trim().toLowerCase()===String(item?.name||"").trim().toLowerCase();
  }
  function invoiceLinks(orderId,item){
    let out=[];
    (Array.isArray(db.projectionInvoices)?db.projectionInvoices:[]).forEach(inv=>{
      (Array.isArray(inv?.items)?inv.items:[]).forEach(it=>{
        if(itemMatches(orderId,item,inv,it))out.push({inv,it,qty:Math.max(0,+it.qty||0)});
      });
    });
    return out;
  }
  function currentProjectionStateScore(){
    let inv=(Array.isArray(db.projectionInvoices)?db.projectionInvoices:[]).length,h=0,refs=0;
    (db.orders||[]).forEach(o=>Object.values(o?.projectionItems||{}).forEach(x=>{
      h+=Array.isArray(x?.deliveryHistory)?x.deliveryHistory.length:0;
      refs+=Array.isArray(x?.invoiceIds)?x.invoiceIds.length:0;
    }));
    return inv*10000+h*10+refs;
  }
  function safetyBackup(){
    try{
      let payload={savedAt:new Date().toISOString(),score:currentProjectionStateScore(),projectionInvoices:clone(Array.isArray(db.projectionInvoices)?db.projectionInvoices:[]),orders:(db.orders||[]).map(o=>({id:o.id,orderNumber:o.orderNumber,client:o.client,clientId:o.clientId,date:o.date,projectionItems:clone(o.projectionItems||{}),projectionInvoiced:o.projectionInvoiced})),finance:clone((db.finance||[]).filter(f=>f?.projectionInvoiceId!=null))};
      let old=null;try{old=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(V9123_BACKUP_KEY)||"null")}catch(e){}
      if(!old||(+payload.score||0)>=(+old.score||0))localStorage.setItem(V9123_BACKUP_KEY,JSON.stringify(payload));
    }catch(e){console.warn("HLGB v91.23 backup da projeção",e)}
  }
  function referencedInvoiceIds(){
    let set=new Set();
    (db.finance||[]).forEach(f=>{if(f?.projectionInvoiceId!=null)set.add(invoiceId(f.projectionInvoiceId))});
    (db.orders||[]).forEach(o=>Object.values(o?.projectionItems||{}).forEach(x=>(x?.invoiceIds||[]).forEach(id=>set.add(invoiceId(id)))));
    return set;
  }
  function restoreFromSafetyBackup(){
    let old=null;try{old=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(V9123_BACKUP_KEY)||"null")}catch(e){}
    if(!old||!Array.isArray(old.projectionInvoices)||!old.projectionInvoices.length)return false;
    db.projectionInvoices=Array.isArray(db.projectionInvoices)?db.projectionInvoices:[];

    return changed;
  }

(function(){

  function clone(v){try{return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v))}catch(e){return v}}
  function invoiceId(v){return String(v==null?"":v)}
  function invoiceDate(inv){return String(inv?.issueDate||inv?.date||"").slice(0,10)}
  function financeForInvoice(id){return (db.finance||[]).find(f=>invoiceId(f.projectionInvoiceId)===invoiceId(id))||null}
  function itemMatches(orderId,item,inv,it){
    let oid=it?.orderId!=null?it.orderId:inv?.orderId;
    if(String(oid??"")!==String(orderId??""))return false;
    if(it?.itemKey!=null&&String(it.itemKey)!=="")return String(it.itemKey)===String(item?.key??"");
    if(item?.productId&&it?.productId)return +it.productId===+item.productId;
    return String(it?.productName||"").trim().toLowerCase()===String(item?.name||"").trim().toLowerCase();
  }
  function invoiceLinks(orderId,item){
    let out=[];
    (Array.isArray(db.projectionInvoices)?db.projectionInvoices:[]).forEach(inv=>{
      (Array.isArray(inv?.items)?inv.items:[]).forEach(it=>{
        if(itemMatches(orderId,item,inv,it))out.push({inv,it,qty:Math.max(0,+it.qty||0)});
      });
    });
    return out;
  }
  function currentProjectionStateScore(){
    let inv=(Array.isArray(db.projectionInvoices)?db.projectionInvoices:[]).length,h=0,refs=0;
    (db.orders||[]).forEach(o=>Object.values(o?.projectionItems||{}).forEach(x=>{
      h+=Array.isArray(x?.deliveryHistory)?x.deliveryHistory.length:0;
      refs+=Array.isArray(x?.invoiceIds)?x.invoiceIds.length:0;
    }));

  }
  function safetyBackup(){
    try{

    }catch(e){console.warn("HLGB v91.23 backup da projeção",e)}

    });
    return changed;
  }

}

function isoDate(d){
let y=d.getFullYear(),m=String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0"),day=String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,"0");
return `${y}-${m}-${day}`;
}
function mondayOf(d){
let x=new Date(d); x.setHours(12,0,0,0);
let day=x.getDay(); let diff=day===0?-6:1-day; x.setDate(x.getDate()+diff); return x;
}
function weekValue(d){
let x=mondayOf(d), jan4=new Date(x.getFullYear(),0,4,12), first=mondayOf(jan4);
let week=Math.round((x-first)/604800000)+1;
return `${x.getFullYear()}-W${String(week).padStart(2,"0")}`;
}
function mondayFromWeek(v){
let [y,w]=v.split("-W").map(Number); if(!y||!w)return mondayOf(new Date());
let jan4=new Date(y,0,4,12), first=mondayOf(jan4), d=new Date(first);d.setDate(d.getDate()+(w-1)*7);return d;
}

function qtyOfOrder(o){
if(Array.isArray(o.grade)&&o.grade.length)return o.grade.reduce((a,x)=>a+(+x.qty||0),0);

let m=String(o.items||"").match(/^\\s*(\\d+)/); return m?+m[1]:0;
}

function setGoalPeriod(mode){
 let s=document.getElementById("goalStart"),e=document.getElementById("goalEnd");
 if(!s||!e)return;
 let now=new Date(),start="",end="";
 if(mode==="day"){
   let d=isoDate(now);s.value=d;e.value=d;renderGoals();return;
 }
 if(mode==="week"){
   let m=mondayOf(now),z=new Date(m);z.setDate(z.getDate()+6);
   start=isoDate(m);end=isoDate(z);
 }else if(mode==="month"){
   start=isoDate(new Date(now.getFullYear(),now.getMonth(),1,12));
   end=isoDate(new Date(now.getFullYear(),now.getMonth()+1,0,12));
 }else if(mode==="year"){
   start=isoDate(new Date(now.getFullYear(),0,1,12));
   end=isoDate(new Date(now.getFullYear(),11,31,12));
 }
 s.value=start;e.value=end;renderGoals();
}
function dateInGoalPeriod(dateValue,start,end){
 let d=String(dateValue||"").slice(0,10);
 if(!d)return false;
 if(start&&d<start)return false;
 if(end&&d>end)return false;
 return true;
}
function goalTargetForPeriod(g,start,end){
 let base=+g.targetValue||0;
 let cadence=g.cadence||"Semanal";
 if(!start&&!end)return base;
 let s=start?new Date(start+"T12:00:00"):new Date();
 let e=end?new Date(end+"T12:00:00"):new Date();
 let days=Math.max(1,Math.floor((e-s)/86400000)+1);
 if(cadence==="Semanal")return base*(days/7);
 if(cadence==="Mensal")return base*(days/30.4375);
 if(cadence==="Anual")return base*(days/365.25);
 return base;
}
function productionValueForGoal(g,start,end){
 // Valor produzido estimado pelos pedidos/produções vinculados ao local/facção.
 // Para facções usa quantidade produzida * preço da facção.
 if(g.scopeType==="Facção"){
   return (db.factions||[])
     .filter(f=>String(f.name||"")===String(g.scopeName||"") && dateInGoalPeriod(f.finishedAt||f.sentAt||f.date,start,end))
     .reduce((a,f)=>a+(+f.done||0)*(+f.price||0),0);
 }
 if(g.scopeType==="Confecção"){
   let loc=(db.productionLocations||[]).find(x=>String(x.name||"")===String(g.scopeName||""));
   if(!loc)return 0;
   return (db.production||[])
     .filter(p=>+p.productionLocationId===+loc.id && dateInGoalPeriod(p.finishedAt||p.date,start,end))
     .reduce((a,p)=>{
       let order=(db.orders||[]).find(o=>+o.id===+p.orderId);
       let unit=order&&qtyOfOrder(order)>0?(+order.total||0)/qtyOfOrder(order):0;
       return a+(+p.done||0)*unit;
     },0);
 }
 if(g.scopeType==="Pessoa"){
   // Pessoa pode ser cortador ou funcionário nominal; usamos cortadores cadastrados.
   let c=(db.cutters||[]).find(x=>String(x.name||"")===String(g.scopeName||""));
   if(!c)return 0;
   return (db.cuts||[])
     .filter(x=>+x.cutterId===+c.id && String(x.status||"").toLowerCase()==="finalizado" && dateInGoalPeriod(x.finishedAt||x.date,start,end))
     .reduce((a,x)=>{
       let order=(db.orders||[]).find(o=>+o.id===+x.orderId);
       let unit=order&&qtyOfOrder(order)>0?(+order.total||0)/qtyOfOrder(order):0;
       return a+(+x.pieces||0)*unit;
     },0);
 }
 return 0;
}

       let order=(db.orders||[]).find(o=>+o.id===+p.orderId);
       let unit=order&&qtyOfOrder(order)>0?(+order.total||0)/qtyOfOrder(order):0;
       return a+(+p.done||0)*unit;
     },0);
 }
 if(g.scopeType==="Pessoa"){
   // Pessoa pode ser cortador ou funcionário nominal; usamos cortadores cadastrados.
   let c=(db.cutters||[]).find(x=>String(x.name||"")===String(g.scopeName||""));
   if(!c)return 0;
   return (db.cuts||[])
     .filter(x=>+x.cutterId===+c.id && String(x.status||"").toLowerCase()==="finalizado" && dateInGoalPeriod(x.finishedAt||x.date,start,end))
     .reduce((a,x)=>{
       let order=(db.orders||[]).find(o=>+o.id===+x.orderId);
       let unit=order&&qtyOfOrder(order)>0?(+order.total||0)/qtyOfOrder(order):0;
       return a+(+x.pieces||0)*unit;
     },0);
 }
 return 0;
}

function setProjectionPeriod(mode){
 let s=document.getElementById("projectionStart"),e=document.getElementById("projectionEnd");
 if(!s||!e)return;
 let now=new Date(),start="",end="";
 if(mode==="week"){
   let m=mondayOf(now),z=new Date(m);z.setDate(z.getDate()+6);
   start=isoDate(m);end=isoDate(z);
 }else if(mode==="month"){
   start=isoDate(new Date(now.getFullYear(),now.getMonth(),1,12));
   end=isoDate(new Date(now.getFullYear(),now.getMonth()+1,0,12));
 }else if(mode==="next30"){
   let z=new Date(now);z.setDate(z.getDate()+30);
   start=isoDate(now);end=isoDate(z);
 }
 s.value=start;e.value=end;
 renderProjection();
}

function projectionItemsForOrder(o){
 let grade=Array.isArray(o?.grade)?o.grade:[];
 let grouped={};
 grade.forEach(it=>{
   let pid=+it.productId||0;
   let p=(db.products||[]).find(x=>+x.id===pid);
   let key=pid?String(pid):("legacy-"+String(p?.name||o.product||o.items||"Produto"));
   if(!grouped[key])grouped[key]={key,productId:pid||null,name:p?.name||o.product||"Produto",qty:0};
   grouped[key].qty+=(+it.qty||0);
 });
 if(!Object.keys(grouped).length){
   let pid=+o.productId||0,p=(db.products||[]).find(x=>+x.id===pid);
   let name=p?.name||cleanFactionProductName?.(o.items||o.product||"Pedido")||o.items||"Pedido";
   grouped[pid?String(pid):"legacy"]={key:pid?String(pid):"legacy",productId:pid||null,name,qty:qtyOfOrder(o)};
 }
 o.projectionItems=(o.projectionItems&&typeof o.projectionItems==="object")?o.projectionItems:{};
 let totalQty=Math.max(1,Object.values(grouped).reduce((a,x)=>a+x.qty,0));
 return Object.values(grouped).map(g=>{
   let saved=o.projectionItems[g.key]||{};
   let unit=0;
   if(g.productId){
     let cId=+o.clientId||0;
     try{unit=typeof suggestedPrice==="function"?+suggestedPrice(cId,g.productId)||0:0}catch(e){}
     if(!unit){let p=(db.products||[]).find(x=>+x.id===g.productId);unit=+p?.price||0}
   }
   let value=unit>0?unit*g.qty:(+o.total||0)*(g.qty/totalQty);
   let legacyInvoiced=!!saved.invoiced;
   let invoicedQty=saved.invoicedQty!=null?Math.max(0,+saved.invoicedQty||0):(legacyInvoiced?g.qty:0);
   invoicedQty=Math.min(g.qty,invoicedQty);
   let remainingQty=Math.max(0,g.qty-invoicedQty);
   return {...g,date:saved.date||"",invoiced:remainingQty<=0,invoiceIds:Array.isArray(saved.invoiceIds)?saved.invoiceIds:[],invoicedQty,remainingQty,value};
 });
}

function finalizeSelectedProjection(){
 let keys=[...document.querySelectorAll(".projectionSelect:checked")].map(x=>x.value);

 let selected=keys.map(v=>{

       clientId:targetClient.id,clientName:targetClient.name,projectionInvoiceId:invoiceId
     });

     invoiceItems.forEach(({ctx,qty})=>{

       let saved=o.projectionItems[item.key]||{};

       let newQty=Math.min(item.qty,prev+qty);
       o.projectionItems[item.key]={
         ...saved,date:item.date,invoicedQty:newQty,invoiced:newQty>=item.qty,
         invoiceIds:[...((saved.invoiceIds)||[]),invoiceId],
         deliveryHistory:[...((saved.deliveryHistory)||[]),{invoiceId,date:issueDate,qty}]

   });

function updateProjectionInvoiceClientPrices(){
 let clientId=+document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceClient")?.value||0;
 let keys=[...document.querySelectorAll(".projectionSelect:checked")].map(x=>x.value);
 let selected=keys.map(v=>{let [oid,key]=String(v).split("::"),o=(db.orders||[]).find(x=>+x.id===+oid);if(!o)return null;let item=projectionItemsForOrder(o).find(i=>String(i.key)===key);return item?{o,item}:null}).filter(Boolean);
 document.querySelectorAll(".projectionInvoiceUnit").forEach(inp=>{
   let i=+inp.dataset.index,ctx=selected[i];if(!ctx)return;
   let unit=0;
   if(ctx.item.productId&&clientId){try{unit=+suggestedPrice(clientId,ctx.item.productId)||0}catch(e){}}
   if(!unit)unit=ctx.item.qty>0?(+ctx.item.value||0)/ctx.item.qty:0;
   inp.value=unit.toFixed(2);
 });
 recalcProjectionInvoiceModal();
}
function toggleProjectionInvoiceTermsDetail(){
 let sel=document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceTerms"),field=document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceTermsDetailField"),label=document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceTermsDetailLabel"),inp=document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceTermsDetail");
 if(!sel||!field)return;
 let value=sel.value||"";
 let show=value==="Personalizado"||value==="Cheque";
 field.style.display=show?"block":"none";
 if(label)label.textContent=value==="Cheque"?"Detalhes dos cheques":"Detalhes da condição personalizada";
 if(inp){
   inp.placeholder=value==="Cheque"?"Ex.: 2 cheques — 15/09 e 30/09":"Ex.: entrada + 2 parcelas, 10/20/30 dias";
   if(!show)inp.value="";
 }
}
function recalcProjectionInvoiceModal(){
 let qty=0,value=0;
 document.querySelectorAll(".projectionInvoiceQty").forEach(inp=>{
   let i=inp.dataset.index,q=Math.max(0,+inp.value||0),u=+document.querySelector(`.projectionInvoiceUnit[data-index="${i}"]`)?.value||0;
   qty+=q;value+=q*u;
 });
 let qel=document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceQtyTotal"),vel=document.getElementById("projectionInvoiceValueTotal");
 if(qel)qel.textContent=qty.toLocaleString("pt-BR");
 if(vel)vel.textContent=money(value);
}

function projectionRemainingQty(item){
 return Math.max(0,item?.remainingQty!=null?(+item.remainingQty||0):((+item?.qty||0)-(+item?.invoicedQty||0)));
}
function projectionUnitForItem(item){
 return (+item?.qty||0)>0?(+item?.value||0)/(+item?.qty||0):0;
}
function projectionRemainingValue(item){
 return projectionRemainingQty(item)*projectionUnitForItem(item);
}
function projectionOpenMissingQty(order,item){
 const oid=String(order?.id??'').trim();
 const pid=String(item?.productId??'').trim();
 if(!oid||!pid)return 0;
 return (db.missingPieces||[]).reduce((sum,m)=>{
   if(String(m?.orderId??'').trim()!==oid)return sum;
   if(String(m?.productId??'').trim()!==pid)return sum;
   if(String(m?.issueType||'Peça faltante')!=='Peça faltante')return sum;
   const allocated=(Array.isArray(m?.history)?m.history:[]).reduce((a,h)=>{
     const q=Math.max(0,+h?.qty||0),dest=String(h?.destination||'');
     if(dest==='finishedPieces')return a+q;
     if(dest==='clientInvoice')return a+Math.max(0,q-Math.max(0,+h?.projectionAppliedQty||0));
     return a;
   },0);
   return sum+Math.max(0,+m?.remainingQty||0)+allocated;
 },0);
}

function projectionRemainingQty(item){
 return Math.max(0,item?.remainingQty!=null?(+item.remainingQty||0):((+item?.qty||0)-(+item?.invoicedQty||0)));
}
function projectionUnitForItem(item){
 return (+item?.qty||0)>0?(+item?.value||0)/(+item?.qty||0):0;
}
function projectionRemainingValue(item){
 return projectionRemainingQty(item)*projectionUnitForItem(item);
}
function projectionOpenMissingQty(order,item){
 const oid=String(order?.id??'').trim();
 const pid=String(item?.productId??'').trim();
 if(!oid||!pid)return 0;
 return (db.missingPieces||[]).reduce((sum,m)=>{
   if(String(m?.orderId??'').trim()!==oid)return sum;
   if(String(m?.productId??'').trim()!==pid)return sum;
   if(String(m?.issueType||'Peça faltante')!=='Peça faltante')return sum;
   const allocated=(Array.isArray(m?.history)?m.history:[]).reduce((a,h)=>{
     const q=Math.max(0,+h?.qty||0),dest=String(h?.destination||'');
     if(dest==='finishedPieces')return a+q;
     if(dest==='clientInvoice')return a+Math.max(0,q-Math.max(0,+h?.projectionAppliedQty||0));
     return a;
   },0);
   return sum+Math.max(0,+m?.remainingQty||0)+allocated;
 },0);
}
function projectionDeliverableQty(order,item){
 return Math.max(0,projectionRemainingQty(item)-projectionOpenMissingQty(order,item));
}
function projectionDeliverableValue(order,item){
 return projectionDeliverableQty(order,item)*projectionUnitForItem(item);
}
function projectionDeliveredRows(startS="0000-01-01",endS="9999-12-31"){
 let out=[];
 (db.projectionInvoices||[]).forEach(inv=>{
   let date=String(inv.issueDate||inv.date||"").slice(0,10);
   if(!date||date<startS||date>endS)return;
   (inv.items||[]).forEach(it=>{
     let o=(db.orders||[]).find(x=>+x.id===+it.orderId)||null;
     out.push({invoice:inv,order:o,item:it,date,qty:+it.qty||0,value:+it.value||((+it.qty||0)*(+it.unitPrice||0))});
   });
 });
 return out;
}
function projectionTrackingMatches(order,productName){
 let clientF=(document.getElementById("projectionClientFilter")?.value||"").trim().toLowerCase();
 let productF=(document.getElementById("projectionProductFilter")?.value||"").trim().toLowerCase();
 let orderF=(document.getElementById("projectionOrderFilter")?.value||"").trim().toLowerCase();
 let displayNo=String(order?(typeof displayOrderNumber==="function"?displayOrderNumber(order):order.id):"").toLowerCase();
 if(clientF&&!String(order?.client||"").toLowerCase().includes(clientF))return false;
 if(productF&&!String(productName||"").toLowerCase().includes(productF))return false;
 if(orderF&&!displayNo.includes(orderF))return false;
 return true;
}

     return [fmtDate(r.date),"#"+String(inv.id||"-"),r.order?"#"+(typeof displayOrderNumber==="function"?displayOrderNumber(r.order):r.order.id):"-",esc(inv.client||r.order?.client||"-"),esc(r.item.productName||"-"),(+r.qty||0).toLocaleString("pt-BR"),money(+r.value||0),esc(cond)];
   })
 ):'<div class="empty">Nenhuma entrega registrada neste período.</div>';
 setProjectionDeliveryTabVisualOnly();
}
function setProjectionDeliveryTabVisualOnly(){

 if(del)del.style.display=projectionDeliveryTab==="delivered"?"block":"none";
 if(br)br.className=projectionDeliveryTab==="remaining"?"primary":"secondary";

}

function projectionProductionSplit(orderId,itemKey){
 let aa=(typeof assignmentsFor==="function"?assignmentsFor(orderId,itemKey):[]);
 if(!aa.length)return "";
 return aa.map(a=>{let l=(db.productionLocations||[]).find(x=>+x.id===+a.locationId);return l?`${l.name}: ${(+a.qty||0).toLocaleString("pt-BR")} pç`:""}).filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
}
function openProjectionProductionSplit(orderId,itemKey){
 if(typeof openCapacityAllocation!=="function"){alert("A divisão de produção não está disponível.");return}
 openCapacityAllocation(orderId,itemKey);
}

 // Mantém as opções dos selects sincronizadas com os dados do período.

 if(locSelect){
   let current=locSelect.value;
   let opts=[...new Set(scheduledBase.map(({order:o,item})=>projectionProductionSplit(o.id,item.key)||projectionLocationForOrder(o)||"Sem local"))].sort((a,b)=>a.localeCompare(b,"pt-BR"));
   locSelect.innerHTML='<option value="">Todos</option>'+opts.map(v=>`<option value="${esc(v)}">${esc(v)}</option>`).join("");
   if(opts.includes(current))locSelect.value=current;
   locationF=locSelect.value||"";
 }
 let statusSelect=document.getElementById("projectionStatusFilter");
 if(statusSelect){
   let current=statusSelect.value;

   if(opts.includes(current))statusSelect.value=current;
   statusF=statusSelect.value||"";
 }

   let displayNo=String(typeof displayOrderNumber==="function"?displayOrderNumber(o):o.id).toLowerCase();
   let loc=projectionProductionSplit(o.id,item.key)||projectionLocationForOrder(o)||"Sem local";
   let st=item.invoiced?"Nota finalizada":(o.status||"Sem status");
   if(clientF&&!String(item.clientName||o.client||"").toLowerCase().includes(clientF))return false;
   if(productF&&!String(item.name||"").toLowerCase().includes(productF))return false;
   if(orderF&&!displayNo.includes(orderF))return false;
   if(locationF&&loc!==locationF)return false;
   if(priorityF&&String(o.priority||"Padrão")!==priorityF)return false;
   if(statusF&&st!==statusF)return false;
   if(minV!=null&&(+item.value||0)<minV)return false;
   if(maxV!=null&&(+item.value||0)>maxV)return false;
   return true;
 });

 let pendingEl=document.getElementById("projectionPendingTable");

   ["Pedido","Cliente","Produto","Entrega do cliente","Peças","Valor","Status","Onde está se

function clearProjectionDetailFilters(){

function drawClientChart(rows){
const canvas=document.getElementById("clientChart"); if(!canvas)return;
const rect=canvas.getBoundingClientRect(); const dpr=window.devicePixelRatio||1;
const w=Math.max(320,rect.width||700),h=260;
canvas.width=w*dpr;canvas.height=h*dpr;canvas.style.height=h+"px";
const ctx=canvas.getContext("2d");ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);
const left=45,right=20,top=20,bottom=58,plotW=w-left-right,plotH=h-top-bottom;
ctx.strokeStyle="#dfe3e8";ctx.fillStyle="#5d6673";ctx.font="12px Arial";
const max=Math.max(...rows.map(r=>r[1].qty),1);
for(let i=0;i<=4;i++){let y=top+plotH-(plotH*i/4);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(left,y);ctx.lineTo(w-right,y);ctx.stroke();let val=Math.round(max*i/4);ctx.fillText(val.toLocaleString("pt-BR"),5,y+4)}
if(!rows.length){ctx.fillText("Nenhuma entrega prevista para a semana.",left,top+30);return}
const gap=Math.max(8,Math.min(22,plotW/(rows.length*5)));
const barW=Math.max(18,(plotW-gap*(rows.length-1))/rows.length);
rows.forEach((r,i)=>{
let x=left+i*(barW+gap), bh=(r[1].qty/max)*plotH,y=top+plotH-bh;
ctx.fillStyle="#17213b";ctx.fillRect(x,y,barW,bh);
ctx.fillStyle="#202631";ctx.textAlign="center";
ctx.fillText(r[1].qty.toLocaleString("pt-BR"),x+barW/2,Math.max(12,y-5));
let label=r[0].length>14?r[0].slice(0,14)+"…":r[0];
ctx.save();ctx.translate(x+barW/2,h-12);ctx.rotate(-Math.PI/7);ctx.fillText(label,0,0);ctx.restore();
});
ctx.textAlign="left";
}

function migratePaymentHistory(){
  db.orders=db.orders||[];
  db.finance=db.finance||[];
  db.invoices=db.invoices||[];
  db.orders.forEach(o=>{
    o.paymentHistory=Array.isArray(o.paymentHistory)?o.paymentHistory:[];
    if(o.paymentHistory.length===0 && (+o.received||0)>0){
      o.paymentHistory.push({
        id:Date.now()+Math.floor(Math.random()*100000),
        date:o.invoiceDate||o.date||isoDate(new Date()),
        method:o.lastPaymentMethod||"PIX",
        value:+(+o.received).toFixed(2),
        note:"Recebimento registrado anteriormente"
      });
    }
    let paid=orderPaid(o), rem=orderRemaining(o);
    o.received=paid;
    o.remaining=rem;
    o.paymentStatus=rem<=0.009?"Pago":(paid>0?"Parcial":"Pendente");

    db.finance.filter(f=>+f.orderId===+o.id&&f.type==="Receber").forEach(f=>{
      f.paid=paid;
      f.remaining=rem;
      f.status=rem<=0.009?"Pago":"Pendente";
    });
    db.invoices.filter(n=>+n.orderId===+o.id).forEach(n=>{
      n.status=rem<=0.009?"Paga":"Pendente";
      n.paidAt=rem<=0.009?(n.paidAt||isoDate(new Date())):null;
    });
  });
  persistDb();
}

function paymentBadge(o){let rem=orderRemaining(o);if(rem<=0.009)return '<span class="badge ok">PAGO</span>';if(orderPaid(o)>0)return '<span class="badge">PARCIAL</span>';return '<span class="badge warn">EM ABERTO</span>'}

    });

async function addPayment(orderId){
 try{
  const o=(db.orders||[]).find(x=>+x.id===+orderId);
  if(!o){alert('Pedido não encontrado.');return}
  o.paymentHistory=Array.isArray(o.paymentHistory)?o.paymentHistory:[];

  const methodEl=document.getElementById('acMethod');
  const valueEl=document.getElementById('acValue');
  const dateEl=document.getElementById('acDate');
  const noteEl=document.getElementById('acNote');
  if(!methodEl||!valueEl||!dateEl){
    alert('O formulário de recebimento não foi carregado corretamente. Feche o acerto e abra novamente.');
    return;
  }

  let value=Number(String(valueEl.value||'').replace(',','.'));
  let rem=orderRemaining(o);
  if(!Number.isFinite(value)||value<=0){alert('Informe um valor maior que zero.');return}
  if(value>rem+0.009){alert('O valor informado é maior que o saldo restante de '+money(rem)+'.');return}

  const method=methodEl.value||'Outro';
  const payment={
    id:Date.now(),
    date:dateEl.value||isoDate(new Date()),
    method,
    value:Number(value.toFixed(2)),
    note:noteEl?noteEl.value||'':'',
    cheque:null
  };

  // Primeiro registra a baixa e salva imediatamente.
  // A foto do cheque, se houver, é processada depois para não atrasar a baixa.
  if(method==='Cheque'){
    const cd=document.getElementById('acChequeDate');
    const co=document.getElementById('acChequeOwner');
    const cn=document.getElementById('acChequeNumber');
    const cp=document.getElementById('acChequePhoto');

    if(!cd?.value){alert('Informe a data do cheque.');return}
    if(!co?.value.trim()){alert('Informe o dono do cheque.');return}

    payment.cheque={
      date:cd.value,
      owner:co.value.trim(),
      number:cn?.value.trim()||'',
      photo:null
    };

    if(cp?.files?.[0] && cp.files[0].size>5*1024*1024){
      alert('A foto do cheque deve ter no máximo 5 MB.');
      return;
    }
  }

  o.paymentHistory.push(payment);
  let newRem=orderRemaining(o);
  o.paymentStatus=newRem<=0.009?'Pago':'Parcial';
  o.received=orderPaid(o);
  o.remaining=newRem;

  db.finance=Array.isArray(db.finance)?db.finance:[];
  let f=db.finance.find(x=>+x.orderId===+o.id&&x.type==='Receber');
  if(f){
    f.paid=orderPaid(o);
    f.remaining=newRem;
    f.status=newRem<=0.009?'Pago':'Pendente';
    f.lastPaymentMethod=payment.method;
  }

  // Atualização imediata, sem renderAll() pesado.
  persistDb();
  renderOrderNotes();
  renderDash();
  renderFinance();
  const historyBox=document.getElementById('paymentHistoryBox');
  if(historyBox) historyBox.innerHTML=paymentMethodRows(o);
  const remainingEl=document.getElementById('acertoRemaining');
  if(remainingEl) remainingEl.textContent=money(newRem);
  if(valueEl) valueEl.value=newRem.toFixed(2);
  if(noteEl) noteEl.value

      const paymentId=payment.id;
      const reader=new FileReader();
      reader.onload=()=>{
        const current=(db.orders||[]).find(x=>+x.id===+orderId);
        const currentPayment=current?.paymentHistory?.find(x=>+x.id===+paymentId);
        if(currentPayment?.cheque){
          currentPayment.cheque.photo=reader.result;
          persistDb();
          const hb=document.getElementById('paymentHistoryBox');
          if(hb) hb.innerHTML=paymentMethodRows(current);
        }
      };
      reader.onerror=()=>console.warn('Não foi possível salvar a foto do cheque.');
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    }
  }
 }catch(e){
  console.error(e);
  alert('Não foi possível dar baixa neste recebimento. O lançamento não foi concluído.');
 }
}

function cleanupOrphanOrderRecords(){
 db.orders=Array.isArray(db.orders)?db.orders:[];
 const validOrderIds=new Set(db.orders.map(o=>+o.id));

 let removed={cuts:0,production:0,separations:0,factions:0,factionPayments:0,missingPieces:0,finance:0};

 function cleanByOrderId(name){
   db[name]=Array.isArray(db[name])?db[name]:[];
   const before=db[name].length;
   db[name]=db[name].filter(x=>{
     if(x.orderId==null || x.orderId==="")return true;
     return validOrderIds.has(+x.orderId);
   });
   removed[name]=before-db[name].length;
 }

 cleanByOrderId("cuts");
 cleanByOrderId("production");
 cleanByOrderId("separations");
 cleanByOrderId("missingPieces");

 // Facções podem estar vinculadas diretamente ao pedido OU à produção.
 db.production=Array.isArray(db.production)?db.production:[];
 const validProductionIds=new Set(db.production.map(p=>+p.id));
 db.factions=Array.isArray(db.factions)?db.factions:[];
 const factionBefore=db.factions.length;
 db.factions=db.factions.filter(f=>{
   if(f.orderId!=null && f.orderId!=="" && !validOrderIds.has(+f.orderId))return false;
   if(f.productionId!=null && f.productionId!=="" && !validProductionIds.has(+f.productionId))return false;
   return true;
 });
 removed.factions=factionBefore-db.factions.length;

 // Pagamentos de facção órfãos
 db.factionPayments=Array.isArray(db.factionPayments)?db.factionPayments:[];
 const validFactionIds=new Set(db.factions.map(f=>+f.id));
 const fpBefore=db.factionPayments.length;
 db.factionPayments=db.factionPayments.filter(p=>{
   if(p.factionServiceId!=null && p.factionServiceId!=="" && !validFactionIds.has(+p.factionServiceId))return false;
   if(p.orderId!=null && p.orderId!=="" && !validOrderIds.has(+p.orderId))return false;
   return true;
 });
 removed.factionPayments=fpBefore-db.factionPayments.length;

 // Financeiro: só remove automaticamente registros explicitamente ligados a um pedido inexistente.
 db.finance=Array.isArray(db.finance)?db.finance:[];
 const finBefore=db.finance.length;
 db.finance=db.finance.filter(f=>{
   if(f.orderId==null || f.orderId==="")return true;
   return validOrderIds.has(+f.orderId);
 });
 removed.finance=finBefore-db.finance.length;

 const total=Object.values(removed).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
 if(total>0){
   try{persistDb()}catch(e){console.error(e)}
   console.info("HLGB: registros órfãos removidos",removed);
 }
 return {total,removed};
}

function setClientReportPeriod(mode){
 let s=document.getElementById("clientReportStart"),e=document.getElementById("clientReportEnd");
 if(!s||!e)return;
 let now=new Date(),start="",end="";
 if(mode==="week"){
   let m=mondayOf(now),z=new Date(m);z.setDate(z.getDate()+6);
   start=isoDate(m);end=isoDate(z);
 }else if(mode==="month"){
   start=isoDate(new Date(now.getFullYear(),now.getMonth(),1,12));
   end=isoDate(new Date(now.getFullYear(),now.getMonth()+1,0,12));
 }else if(mode==="year"){
   start=isoDate(new Date(now.getFullYear(),0,1,12));
   end=isoDate(new Date(now.getFullYear(),11,31,12));
 }
 s.value=start;e.value=end;
 renderClientPotentialReport();
}
function clientOrderCost(o){
 let cost=0;
 // Prefer detailed order items when available.
 if(Array.isArray(o.lines)&&o.lines.length){
   o.lines.forEach(line=>{
     let p=(db.products||[]).find(x=>+x.id===+line.productId);
     if(!p)return;
     let mat=(p.materials||[]).reduce((s,m)=>s+materialCostPerPiece(m),0);
     let unitCost=mat+(+p.labor||0);
     cost+=unitCost*(+line.qty||0);
   });
   return cost;
 }
 let p=(db.products||[]).find(x=>+x.id===+o.productId);
 if(!p)return 0;
 let mat=(p.materials||[]).reduce((s,m)=>s+materialCostPerPiece(m),0);
 let unitCost=mat+(+p.labor||0);
 return unitCost*qtyOfOrder(o);
}
function clientPaidAmount(clientName,start,end){
 let name=String(clientName||"").toLowerCase();
 let total=0;

 // Acertos/recebimentos vinculados a pedidos/clientes, quando existirem.
 if(Array.isArray(db.receipts)){
   db.receipts.forEach(r=>{
     let d=r.date||r.paymentDate||"";
     let rn=String(r.client||r.clientName||"").toLowerCase();
     if(rn===name && (!start||d>=start)&&(!end||d<=end))total+=+r.value||0;
   });
 }

 // Lançamentos financeiros recebidos: usa client/clientName quando houver;
 // para dados antigos, tenta associação pelo nome na descrição.
 (db.finance||[]).forEach(f=>{
   if(f.type!=="Receber"||!["Pago","Parcial"].includes(f.status))return;
   let d=f.paidDate||f.paymentDate||f.date||"";
   if((start&&d<start)||(end&&d>end))return;
   let fn=String(f.client||f.clientName||"").toLowerCase();
   let desc=String(f.desc||"").toLowerCase();
   if(fn===name || (!fn&&name&&desc.includes(name))){
     if(Array.isArray(f.paymentHistory)&&f.paymentHistory.length){
       total+=f.paymentHistory.filter(h=>(!start||h.date>=start)&&(!end||h.date<=end)).reduce((a,h)=>a+(+h.value||0),0);
     }else total+=(f.status==="Parcial"?(+f.paid||0):(+f.value||0));
   }
 });

 // Notas/pedidos podem armazenar received/paidAmount.
 (db.orders||[]).forEach(o=>{
   if(String(o.client||"").toLowerCase()!==name)return;
   let d=o.date||"";
   if((start&&d<start)||(end&&d>end))return;
   if(o.paidAmount!=null)total+=+o.paidAmount||0;
   else if(o.received!=null)total+=+o.received||0;
 });
 return total;
}

}

async function addPayment(orderId){
 try{
  const o=(db.orders||[]).find(x=>+x.id===+orderId);
  if(!o){alert('Pedido não encontrado.');return}
  o.paymentHistory=Array.isArray(o.paymentHistory)?o.paymentHistory:[];

  const methodEl=document.getElementById('acMethod');
  const valueEl=document.getElementById('acValue');
  const dateEl=document.getElementById('acDate');
  const noteEl=document.getElementById('acNote');
  if(!methodEl||!valueEl||!dateEl){
    alert('O formulário de recebimento não foi carregado corretamente. Feche o acerto e abra novamente.');
    return;
  }

  let value=Number(String(valueEl.value||'').replace(',','.'));
  let rem=orderRemaining(o);
  if(!Number.isFinite(value)||value<=0){alert('Informe um valor maior que zero.');return}
  if(value>rem+0.009){alert('O valor informado é maior que o saldo restante de '+money(rem)+'.');return}

  const method=methodEl.value||'Outro';
  const payment={
    id:Date.now(),
    date:dateEl.value||isoDate(new Date()),
    method,
    value:Number(value.toFixed(2)),
    note:noteEl?noteEl.value||'':'',
    cheque:null
  };

  // Primeiro registra a baixa e salva imediatamente.
  // A foto do cheque, se houver, é processada depois para não atrasar a baixa.
  if(method==='Cheque'){
    const cd=document.getElementById('acChequeDate');
    const co=document.getElementById('acChequeOwner');
    const cn=document.getElementById('acChequeNumber');
    const cp=document.getElementById('acChequePhoto');

    if(!cd?.value){alert('Informe a data do cheque.');return}
    if(!co?.value.trim()){alert('Informe o dono do cheque.');return}

    payment.cheque={
      date:cd.value,
      owner:co.value.trim(),
      number:cn?.value.trim()||'',
      photo:null
    };

    if(cp?.files?.[0] && cp.files[0].size>5*1024*1024){
      alert('A foto do cheque deve ter no máximo 5 MB.');
      return;
    }
  }

  o.paymentHistory.push(payment);
  let newRem=orderRemaining(o);
  o.paymentStatus=newRem<=0.009?'Pago':'Parcial';
  o.received=orderPaid(o);
  o.remaining=newRem;

  db.finance=Array.isArray(db.finance)?db.finance:[];
  let f=db.finance.find(x=>+x.orderId===+o.id&&x.type==='Receber');
  if(f){
    f.paid=orderPaid(o);
    f.remaining=newRem;
    f.status=newRem<=0.009?'Pago':'Pendente';
    f.lastPaymentMethod=payment.method;
  }

  // Atualização imediata, sem renderAll() pesado.
  persistDb();
  renderOrderNotes();
  renderDash();
  renderFinance();
  const historyBox=document.getElementById('paymentHistoryBox');
  if(historyBox) historyBox.innerHTM

--- occurrence 3 @ 259570 ---
a foi gerada ainda.</div>';
 ["orderNotesTable","allOrderNotesTable"].forEach(id=>{let tbl=document.getElementById(id);if(tbl)tbl.innerHTML=id==="orderNotesTable"?openHtml:allHtml});
}

  let rem=orderRemaining(o);
  if(!Number.isFinite(value)||value<=0){alert('Informe um valor maior que zero.');return}

    cheque:null

  // Primeiro registra a baixa e salva imediatamente.
  // A foto do cheque, se houver, é processada depois para não atrasar a baixa.
  if(method==='Cheque'){
    const cd=document.getElementById('acChequeDate');
    const co=document.getElementById('acChequeOwner');
    const cn=document.getElementById('acChequeNumber');
    const cp=document.getElementById('acChequePhoto');

    if(!cd?.value){alert('Informe a data do cheque.');return}
    if(!co?.value.trim()){alert('Informe o dono do cheque.');return}

    payment.cheque={
      date:cd.value,
      owner:co.value.trim(),
      number:cn?.value.trim()||'',
      photo:null
    };

    if(cp?.files?.[0] && cp.files[0].size>5*1024*1024){
      alert('A foto do cheque deve ter no máximo 5 MB.');
      return;
    }
  }

  o.paymentHistory.push(payment);
  let newRem=orderRemaining(o);
  o.paymentStatus=newRem<=0.009?'Pago':'Parcial';
  o.received=orderPaid(o);
  o.remaining=newRem;

  db.finance=Array.isArray(db.finance)?db.finance:[];
  let f=db.finance.find(x=>+x.orderId===+o.id&&x.type==='Receber');
  if(f){
    f.paid=orderPaid(o);
    f.remaining=newRem;
    f.status=newRem<=0.009?'Pago':'Pendente';
    f.lastPaymentMethod=payment.method;
  }

  // Atualização imediata, sem renderAll() pesado.
  persistDb();
  renderOrderNotes();
  renderDash();
  renderFinance();
  const historyBox=document.getElementById('paymentHistoryBox');
  if(historyBox) historyBox.innerHTML=paymentMethodRows(o);
  const remainingEl=document.getElementById('acertoRemaining');
  if(remainingEl) remainingEl.textContent=money(newRem);
  if(valueEl) valueEl.value=newRem.toFixed(2);
  if(noteEl) noteEl.value='';
  const saveFeedback=document.getElementById('acertoSaveFeedback');
  if(saveFeedback){
    saveFeedback.textContent='✓ Recebimento salvo com sucesso.';
    saveFeedback.style.display='block';
  }

  // Se houver foto do cheque, ela é salva em segundo plano.
  if(method==='Cheque'){
    const cp=document.getElementById('acChequePhoto');
    if(cp?.files?.[0]){
      const file=cp.files[0];
      const paymentId=payment.id;
      const reader=new FileReader();
      reader.onload=()=>{
        const current=(db.orders||[]).find(x=>+x.id===+orderId);
        const currentPayment=current?.paymentHistory?.find(x=>+x.id===+paymentId);
        if(currentPayment?.cheque){
          currentPayment.cheque.photo=reader.result;
          persistDb();
          const hb=document.getElementById('paymentHistoryBox');
          if(hb) hb.innerHTML=paymentMethodRows(current);
        }
      };
      reader.onerror=()=>console.warn('Não foi possível salvar a foto do cheque.');
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    }
  }
 }catch(e){
  console.error(e);
  alert('Não foi possível dar baixa neste recebimento. O lançamento não foi concluído.');
 }
}

async function addPayment(orderId){
 try{
  const o=(db.orders||[]).find(x=>+x.id===+orderId);
  if(!o){alert('Pedido não encontrado.');return}
  o.paymentHistory=Array.isArray(o.paymentHistory)?o.paymentHistory:[];

  const methodEl=document.getElementById('acMethod');
  const valueEl=document.getElementById('acValue');
  const dateEl=document.getElementById('acDate');
  const noteEl=document.getElementById('acNote');
  if(!methodEl||!valueEl||!dateEl){
    alert('O formulário de recebimento não foi carregado corretamente. Feche o acerto e abra novamente.');
    return;
  }

  let value=Number(String(valueEl.value||'').replace(',','.'));
  let rem=orderRemaining(o);
  if(!Number.isFinite(value)||value<=0){alert('Informe um valor maior que zero.');return}
  if(value>rem+0.009){alert('O valor informado é maior que o saldo restante de '+money(rem)+'.');return}

  const method=methodEl.value||'Outro';
  const payment={
    id:Date.now(),
    date:dateEl.value||isoDate(new Date()),
    method,
    value:Number(value.toFixed(2)),
    note:noteEl?noteEl.value||'':'',
    cheque:null
  };

      date:cd.value,

      photo:null
    };

      alert('A foto do cheque deve ter no máximo 5 MB.');
      return;

function fmtDate(v){ if(!v) return "-"; let d=new Date(String(v)+"T12:00:00"); return isNaN(d)?String(v):d.toLocaleDateString("pt-BR"); }
function noteDueInfo(o){
  let raw=o.dueDate||o.date;
  if(!raw) return {label:"-",cls:""};
  let d=new Date(raw+"T12:00:00");
  if(isNaN(d)) return {label:fmtDate(raw),cls:""};
  let today=new Date(); today.setHours(12,0,0,0);
  let diff=Math.round((d-today)/86400000);
  if(diff<0) return {label:`Vencida há ${Math.abs(diff)} dia${Math.abs(diff)===1?"":"s"}`,cls:"warn"};
  if(diff===0) return {label:"Vence hoje",cls:"warn"};
  return {label:`Vence em ${diff} dia${diff===1?"":"s"}`,cls:""};
}
function orderPaid(o){
  let arr=Array.isArray(o.paymentHistory)?o.paymentHistory:[];
  if(arr.length) return arr.reduce((s,p)=>s+(+p.value||0),0);
  // Compatibilidade com versões anteriores que guardavam o recebido
  // diretamente no pedido, sem registrar o histórico.
  return Math.max(0,+o.received||0);
}
function orderRemaining(o){
  return Math.max(0,(+o.total||0)-orderPaid(o));
}

function migratePaymentHistory(){
  db.orders=db.orders||[];
  db.finance=db.finance||[];
  db.invoices=db.invoices||[];
  db.orders.forEach(o=>{
    o.paymentHistory=Array.isArray(o.paymentHistory)?o.paymentHistory:[];
    if(o.paymentHistory.length===0 && (+o.received||0)>0){
      o.paymentHistory.push({
        id:Date.now()+Math.floor(Math.random()*100000),
        date:o.invoiceDate||o.date||isoDate(new Date()),
        method:o.lastPaymentMethod||"PIX",
        value:+(+o.received).toFixed(2),
        note:"Recebimento registrado anteriormente"
      });
    }
    let paid=orderPaid(o), rem=orderRemaining(o);
    o.received=paid;
    o.remaining=rem;
    o.paymentStatus=rem<=0.009?"Pago":(paid>0?"Parcial":"Pendente");

    db.finance.filter(f=>+f.orderId===+o.id&&f.type==="Receber").forEach(f=>{
      f.paid=paid;
      f.remaining=rem;
      f.status=rem<=0.009?"Pago":"Pendente";
    });
    db.invoices.filter(n=>+n.orderId===+o.id).forEach(n=>{
      n.status=rem<=0.009?"Paga":"Pendente";
      n.paidAt=rem<=0.009?(n.paidAt||isoDate(new Date())):null;
    });
  });
  persistDb();
}

function paymentBadge(o){let rem=orderRemaining(o);if(rem<=0.009)return '<span class="badge ok">PAGO</span>';if(orderPaid(o)>0)return '<span class="badge">PARCIAL</span>';return '<span class="badge warn">EM ABERTO</span>'}

function fmtDate(v){ if(!v) return "-"; let d=new Date(String(v)+"T12:00:00"); return isNaN(d)?String(v):d.toLocaleDateString("pt-BR"); }
function noteDueInfo(o){
  let raw=o.dueDate||o.date;
  if(!raw) return {label:"-",cls:""};
  let d=new Date(raw+"T12:00:00");
  if(isNaN(d)) return {label:fmtDate(raw),cls:""};
  let today=new Date(); today.setHours(12,0,0,0);
  let diff=Math.round((d-today)/86400000);
  if(diff<0) return {label:`Vencida há ${Math.abs(diff)} dia${Math.abs(diff)===1?"":"s"}`,cls:"warn"};
  if(diff===0) return {label:"Vence hoje",cls:"warn"};
  return {label:`Vence em ${diff} dia${diff===1?"":"s"}`,cls:""};
}
function orderPaid(o){
  let arr=Array.isArray(o.paymentHistory)?o.paymentHistory:[];
  if(arr.length) return arr.reduce((s,p)=>s+(+p.value||0),0);
  // Compatibilidade com versões anteriores que guardavam o recebido
  // diretamente no pedido, sem registrar o histórico.
  return Math.max(0,+o.received||0);
}
function orderRemaining(o){
  return Math.max(0,(+o.total||0)-orderPaid(o));
}

    o.paymentHistory=Array.isArray(o.paymentHistory)?o.paymentHistory:[];
    if(o.paymentHistory.length===0 && (+o.received||0)>0){
      o.paymentHistory.push({
        id:Date.now()+Math.floor(Math.random()*100000),
        date:o.invoiceDate||o.date||isoDate(new Date()),
        method:o.lastPaymentMethod||"PIX",
        value:+(+o.received).toFixed(2),
        note:"Recebimento registrado anteriormente"
      });
    }
    let paid=orderPaid(o), rem=orderRemaining(o);
    o.received=paid;
    o.remaining=rem;
    o.paymentStatus=rem<=0.009?"Pago":(paid>0?"Parcial":"Pendente");

    db.finance.filter(f=>+f.orderId===+o.id&&f.type==="Receber").forEach(f=>{
      f.paid=paid;
      f.remaining=rem;
      f.status=rem<=0.009?"Pago":"Pendente";
    });
    db.invoices.filter(n=>+n.orderId===+o.id).forEach(n=>{
      n.status=rem<=0.009?"Paga":"Pendente";
      n.paidAt=rem<=0.009?(n.paidAt||isoDate(new Date())):null;
    });
  });
  persistDb();
}

function fmtDate(v){ if(!v) return "-"; let d=new Date(String(v)+"T12:00:00"); return isNaN(d)?String(v):d.toLocaleDateString("pt-BR"); }
function noteDueInfo(o){
  let raw=o.dueDate||o.date;
  if(!raw) return {label:"-",cls:""};
  let d=new Date(raw+"T12:00:00");
  if(isNaN(d)) return {label:fmtDate(raw),cls:""};
  let today=new Date(); today.setHours(12,0,0,0);
  let diff=Math.round((d-today)/86400000);
  if(diff<0) return {label:`Vencida há ${Math.abs(diff)} dia${Math.abs(diff)===1?"":"s"}`,cls:"warn"};
  if(diff===0) return {label:"Vence hoje",cls:"warn"};
  return {label:`Vence em ${diff} dia${diff===1?"":"s"}`,cls:""};
}
function orderPaid(o){
  let arr=Array.isArray(o.paymentHistory)?o.paymentHistory:[];
  if(arr.length) return arr.reduce((s,p)=>s+(+p.value||0),0);
  // Compatibilidade com versões anteriores que guardavam o recebido
  // diretamente no pedido, sem registrar o histórico.
  return Math.max(0,+o.received||0);
}
function orderRemaining(o){
  return Math.max(0,(+o.total||0)-orderPaid(o));
}

function migratePaymentHistory(){
  db.orders=db.orders||[];
  db.finance=db.finance||[];
  db.invoices=db.invoices||[];
  db.orders.forEach(o=>{
    o.paymentHistory=Array.isArray(o.paymentHistory)?o.paymentHistory:[];
    if(o.paymentHistory.length===0 && (+o.received||0)>0){
      o.paymentHistory.push({
        id:Date.now()+Math.floor(Math.random()*100000),
        date:o.invoiceDate||o.date||isoDate(new Date()),
        method:o.lastPaymentMethod||"PIX",
        value:+(+o.received).toFixed(2),
        note:"Recebimento registrado anteriormente"
      });
    }
    let paid=orderPaid(o), rem=orderRemaining(o);
    o.received=paid;
    o.remaining=rem;
    o.paymentStatus=rem<=0.009?"Pago":(paid>0?"Parcial":"Pendente");

    db.finance.filter(f=>+f.orderId===+o.id&&f.type==="Receber").forEach(f=>{
      f.paid=paid;
      f.remaining=rem;
      f.status=rem<=0.009?"Pago":"Pendente";

    db.invoices.filter(n=>+n.orderId===+o.id).forEach(n=>{
      n.status=rem<=0.009?"Paga":"Pendente";
      n.paidAt=rem<=0.009?(n.paidAt||isoDate(new Date())):null;
    });
  });
  persistDb();
}

function migratePaymentHistory(){
  db.orders=db.orders||[];
  db.finance=db.finance||[];
  db.invoices=db.invoices||[];
  db.orders.forEach(o=>{
    o.paymentHistory=Array.isArray(o.paymentHistory)?o.paymentHistory:[];
    if(o.paymentHistory.length===0 && (+o.received||0)>0){
      o.paymentHistory.push({
        id:Date.now()+Math.floor(Math.random()*100000),
        date:o.invoiceDate||o.date||isoDate(new Date()),
        method:o.lastPaymentMethod||"PIX",
        value:+(+o.received).toFixed(2),
        note:"Recebimento registrado anteriormente"
      });
    }
    let paid=orderPaid(o), rem=orderRemaining(o);
    o.received=paid;
    o.remaining=rem;
    o.paymentStatus=rem<=0.009?"Pago":(paid>0?"Parcial":"Pendente");

    db.finance.filter(f=>+f.orderId===+o.id&&f.type==="Receber").forEach(f=>{
      f.paid=paid;
      f.remaining=rem;
      f.status=rem<=0.009?"Pago":"Pendente";
    });
    db.invoices.filter(n=>+n.orderId===+o.id).forEach(n=>{
      n.status=rem<=0.009?"Paga":"Pendente";
      n.paidAt=rem<=0.009?(n.paidAt||isoDate(new Date())):null;
    });
  });
  persistDb();
}

function paymentBadge(o){let rem=orderRemaining(o);if(rem<=0.009)return '<span class="badge ok">PAGO</span>';if(orderPaid(o)>0)return '<span class="badge">PARCIAL</span>';return '<span class="badge warn">EM ABERTO</span>'}
function renderOrderNotes(){
 let ready=(db.orders||[]).filter(o=>o.invoiceReady===true || o.noteReady===true || (db.invoices||[]).some(n=>+n.orderId===+o.id));
 ready.forEach(o=>{o.paymentHistory=o.paymentHistory||[]});
 let openList=ready.filter(o=>orderRemaining(o)>0.009);
 let open=openList.length;
 let paid=ready.filter(o=>orderRemaining(o)<=0.009).length;
 let total=ready.reduce((s,o)=>s+(+o.total||0),0);
 let openValue=openList.reduce((s,o)=>s+orderRemaining(o),0);
 let overdue=openList.filter(o=>{let x=noteDueInfo(o);return x.cls==="warn" && String(x.label).startsWith("Vencida")}).length;
 let summaryHtml=`<div class="card"><small>Notas em aberto</small><strong>${open}</strong></div><div class="card"><small>Notas vencidas</small><strong>${overdue}</strong></div><div class="card"><small>Valor em aberto</small><strong>${money(openValue)}</strong></div><div class="card"><small>Total das notas</small><strong>${money(total)}</strong></div>`;
 ["orderNotesSummary","allOrderNotesSummary"].forEach(id=>{let sum=document.getElementById(id);if(sum)sum.innerHTML=summaryHtml});
 let openHtml=openList.length?table(["Cliente","Pedido","Total","Recebido","Restante","Data da cobrança","Vencimento","Condição de pagamento","Situação","Ações"],openList.map(o=>{
   let info=noteDueInfo(o), paidValue=orderPaid(o), rem=orderRemaining(o);
   return [esc(o.client||""),"#"+o.id,money(o.total),money(paidValue),money(rem),fmtDate(o.invoiceDate||o.date),fmtDate(o.dueDate||o.date),esc(o.paymentTerms||"Não informado"),`<span class="badge ${info.cls}">${esc(info.label)}</span>`,
   `<button class="primary" onclick="openAcerto(${o.id})">💰 Acerto</button>
    <button class="secondary" onclick="notaPronta(${o.id})">🖨️ Imprimir</button>
    <button class="danger" onclick="deleteNote(${o.id})">Excluir nota</button>`]
 })): '<div class="empty">Nenhuma nota em aberto.</div>';
 let allHtml=ready.length?table(["Cliente","Pedido","Total","Recebido","Restante","Vencimento","Condição de pagamento","Status","Ações"],ready.map(o=>{
   let rem=orderRemaining(o), paidValue=orderPaid(o);
   return [esc(o.client||""),"#"+o.id,money(o.total),money(paidValue),money(rem),fmtDate(o.dueDate||o.date),esc(o.paymentTerms||"Não informado"),paymentBadge(o),
   `<button class="primary" onclick="openAcerto(${o.id})">💰 Acerto</button>
   <button class="secondary" onclick="notaPronta(${o.id})">🖨️ Imprimir</button>

  return {label:`Vence em ${diff} dia${diff===1?"":"s"}`,cls:""};
}
function orderPaid(o){
  let arr=Array.isArray(o.paymentHistory)?o.paymentHistory:[];
  if(arr.length) return arr.reduce((s,p)=>s+(+p.value||0),0);
  // Compatibilidade com versões anteriores que guardavam o recebido
  // diretamente no pedido, sem registrar o histórico.
  return Math.max(0,+o.received||0);
}
function orderRemaining(o){
  return Math.max(0,(+o.total||0)-orderPaid(o));
}

function paymentMethodRows(o){
 let arr=o.paymentHistory||[];
 if(!arr.length)return '<div class="empty">Nenhum pagamento lançado ainda.</div>';
 return table(["Data","Forma","Valor","Detalhes","Ação"],arr.map((p,i)=>{
  let det=esc(p.note||'-');
  if(p.method==='Cheque'){const c=p.cheque||{};det=`Cheque${c.number?' nº '+esc(c.number):''}${c.owner?' — '+esc(c.owner):''}${c.date?' — data '+fmtDate(c.date):''}${c.photo?` <button class="secondary" type="button" onclick="viewChequePhoto(${o.id},${i})">📷 Foto</button>`:''}${p.note?`<br>${esc(p.note)}`:''}`;}
  return [fmtDate(p.date),esc(p.method),money(p.value),det,`<button class="danger" onclick="removePayment(${o.id},${i})">Excluir</button>`];
 }));
}
